TL;DR: HyperLoRA是一种用于联邦微调的统一框架，通过超网络驱动的LoRA生成和乘积空间聚合，解决了现有方法中的结构聚合偏差和客户端初始化滞后问题，显著提升了收敛速度、鲁棒性和个性化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦LoRA方法存在结构聚合偏差和客户端侧初始化滞后两个根本性缺陷，导致收敛慢和聚合不准确，难以有效适应分布式异构数据环境。

Method: 提出HyperLoRA框架：客户端使用学习到的生成器根据数据分布特征生成LoRA参数，实现适应过程的摊销；服务器端引入学习型聚合模块，在低秩乘积空间直接合成更新，并辅以轻量级残差校正模块提升在非独立同分布下的稳定性。

Result: 在多个联邦视觉与视觉-语言基准实验中，HyperLoRA相比已有方法实现了更快的收敛速度、更强的分布偏移鲁棒性以及更优的个性化性能。

Conclusion: HyperLoRA通过将迭代优化和启发式聚合替换为可学习算子，统一解决了联邦LoRA中的关键挑战，为高效、个性化且快速收敛的联邦微调提供了新范式。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning of foundation models using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) offers a communication efficient solution for distributed learning. However, existing federated LoRA methods suffer from two fundamental limitations: (1) structural aggregation bias, where independently averaging low rank factors fails to approximate the true combined update, and (2) client side initialization lag, as clients repeatedly reinitialize LoRA parameters across communication rounds, slowing convergence. We propose HyperLoRA, a unified framework that addresses both issues through amortized federated adaptation through hypernetwork-driven LoRA generation and product space aggregation. Instead of iterative per-client optimization, HyperLoRA employs a learned generator that maps client distribution signatures to LoRA initializations, effectively amortizing per client adaptation. On the server side, we introduce a learned aggregation module that directly synthesizes updates in the low-rank product space, eliminating the inconsistencies of factor-wise averaging. A lightweight residual correction module further improves stability under heterogenous (non-IID) client distributions.By replacing iterative optimization and heuristic averaging with learned operators, HyperLoRA jointly enables efficient personalization, unbiased aggregation, and faster convergence. Experiments on federated vision and vision-language benchmarks show that HyperLoRA achieves improved convergence speed, greater robustness to distribution shift, and stronger personalization performance compared to prior federated LoRA methods.

</details>


### [223] [Where does Absolute Position come from in decoder-only Transformers?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06160)
*Valeria Ruscio,Umberto Nanni,Fabrizio Silvestri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RoPE-trained transformers inadvertently reveal absolute position information due to architectural features like the causal mask and residual stream, with attention sinks acting as token-anchored stabilizers.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To understand how RoPE-trained transformers capture absolute positional information despite using relative position encoding.

Method: Analyzed attention patterns in RoPE-trained transformers, identifying contributions from the causal mask and residual stream across different architectures.

Result: Found that the causal mask and residual stream introduce absolute position leakage; NTK scaling, sliding-window attention, and standard RoPE modulate this effect differently; replacing the BOS embedding reduces residual-stream effects by 40%.

Conclusion: Absolute position information emerges in RoPE models not from RoPE itself but from architectural choices, particularly the causal mask and residual stream dynamics involving attention sinks.

Abstract: RoPE-trained transformers distinguish absolute position in their attention patterns, even though RoPE encodes only relative offsets in the inner product. We trace this leakage to two architectural components, The causal mask is responsible for the first: its per-query softmax denominator depends on the absolute query position by construction. The residual stream supplies the second. Under causal attention the activation at position $0$ attends only to itself and runs as a closed dynamical system from the embedding of the token at that position; downstream attention reads this trajectory through sink-reading heads. Both components appear in all three architectures we study, in architecturally specific balance: NTK scaling suppresses the residual-stream component, sliding-window attention allows it to accumulate with depth, and standard RoPE sits between. Replacing the \texttt{BOS} embedding before the forward pass removes $40\%$ of the residual-stream component at early queries. Attention sinks are token-anchored stabilizers that pass forward a deterministic fingerprint of the token at position $0$, constant across inputs when that token is the auto-prepended \texttt{BOS} and varying with it otherwise.

</details>


### [224] [ProSarc: Prosody-Aware Sarcasm Recognition Framework via Temporal Prosodic Incongruity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06168)
*Prathamjyot Singh,Ashima Sood,Sahil Sharma,Jasmeet Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProSarc是一种基于音频的讽刺检测框架，通过建模时间性韵律不一致性（即局部韵律动态与语句级情感基线之间的不匹配）来识别讽刺。该方法在多个数据集上表现优越，并能估计模型不确定性及定位讽刺起始点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频讽刺检测方法未能充分建模韵律不一致性这一关键特征，而人类识别讽刺常依赖于语调与整体情感基调的矛盾。因此，需要一种能够显式建模这种时序不一致性的自动化方法。

Method: 提出ProSarc框架，包含两条编码路径：全局情感编码器和时序韵律编码器（BiLSTM+多头注意力），二者输出送入韵律不一致性分析模块生成不一致性得分用于分类；采用蒙特卡洛dropout提供不确定性估计，并利用注意力机制无监督地定位讽刺起始时刻。

Result: 在MUStARD++上F1达75.3，优于以往纯音频方法；在PodSarc（F1=62.9）和MuSaG（F1=65.6）上展现良好泛化能力；十次验证表明不一致性建模显著有效（Wilcoxon p=0.002, Cohen's d=1.51）；人类评估证实模型不确定性反映感知模糊性，预测的讽刺起始时间与人工标注窗口一致。

Conclusion: 显式建模韵律不一致性可有效提升音频讽刺检测性能，ProSarc不仅准确分类讽刺，还能提供可解释的不确定性估计和时序定位，具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: We present ProSarc, an audio-only framework that detects sarcasm by modelling temporal prosodic incongruity, that is, the mismatch between local prosodic dynamics and the utterance-level emotional baseline. Dual encoding paths, a Global Emotion Encoder and a Temporal Prosody Encoder (BiLSTM + multi-head attention), feed a Prosodic Incongruity Analyzer that produces a scalar incongruity score for classification. Monte Carlo dropout provides uncertainty estimates, and an attention-based mechanism localises sarcastic onset without frame-level labels. ProSarc outperforms prior audio-only methods on MUStARD++ (F1=75.3) and generalises to spontaneous (PodSarc, F1=62.9) and cross-lingual speech (MuSaG, F1=65.6). Ten-run validation confirms the contribution of incongruity modelling (Wilcoxon p=0.002, Cohen's d=1.51). Human evaluation shows that model uncertainty tracks perceptual ambiguity and predicted onsets align with human-annotated temporal windows.

</details>


### [225] [Learning to replenish: A hybrid deep reinforcement learning for dynamic inventory management in the pharmaceutical supply chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06201)
*Amandeep Kaur,Gyan Prakash*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于深度强化学习的混合A3C-DPPO算法，用于优化具有不确定需求和可变供应链条件的药品库存补货策略，以在保证高服务水平的同时最大化供应链利润。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药品供应链面临需求波动、补货周期变化及产品保质期短等挑战，导致库存管理复杂，传统方法难以兼顾库存成本与供应稳定性，因此需要智能化的动态补货策略。

Method: 将库存优化问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程，设计一种适用于连续动作空间的混合异步优势演员评论家-分布式近端策略优化（A3C-DPPO）算法，并使用真实药品库存数据进行数值验证。

Result: 所提算法在动态环境下能自适应调整补货策略，相比多种基准方法显著降低了库存成本，且具备实际应用可行性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了深度强化学习在复杂药品供应链库存管理中的有效性，为应对不确定性提供了高性能的智能决策方案。

Abstract: Pharmaceutical supply chains (PSCs) struggle with inventory management (IM) due to unpredictable demand patterns and variable lead times associated with restocking. This complexity is further compounded by the finite shelf lives of pharmaceutical products, which necessitate a delicate balance between adequate stock and minimal waste. These intertwined factors create a complex optimization problem that requires sophisticated inventory strategies to ensure both product availability and PSC efficiency. This study aims to develop an optimal inventory replenishment policy for pharmaceutical products that can handle the stochasticity arising from uncertain demand and variable PSC conditions. The objective is to maximize the profitability of the PSC while maintaining a high patient service level. We formulate the problem as a Markov decision process and propose a deep reinforcement learning (DRL) approach, specifically, a hybrid asynchronous advantage actor critic distributed proximal policy optimization (A3C DPPO)algorithm. The A3C DPPO algorithm is tailored to handle the continuous action space inherent in IM. The numerical results demonstrate that the proposed algorithm adaptively updates the inventory replenishment strategy under dynamic scenarios, resulting in lower inventory costs compared to various benchmarks. We also conduct numerical validation using real-world pharmaceutical inventory data to confirm the practical feasibility of the proposed algorithm.

</details>


### [226] [Unsupervised Pattern Analysis in Japanese Veterinary Toxicology: A Regulatory-Compliant Framework for Cross-Species Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06207)
*Yukiko Kawakami,Mohammad Shirazi,Ryo Shimizuwa,Saito Shinoda,Alireza Mortazavi,Matsumoto Kawahara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种结合监管框架的无监督分析方法，利用日本国家兽药检测实验室数据库发现区域特异性的药物毒性模式，揭示了不同动物物种间的肝、肾及皮肤毒性聚集特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有兽药药物警戒系统在捕捉受地区生物和监管环境影响的特异性毒性模式方面存在不足，尤其是在日本，物种代谢差异和特定报告制度加剧了这一问题。因此需要一种更具解释性和适应本地监管背景的分析框架。

Method: 将不良事件编码为器官系统对齐的表示形式，并校正物种特异性报告偏差；采用基于相似性的聚类和降维技术，在NVAL数据库（4,120份高置信度报告）中进行潜在毒性结构挖掘，并使用余弦相似度等指标评估聚类效果。

Result: 识别出三个显著的物种聚类（p < 0.01），包括伴侣动物以肝脏毒性为主（0.42 ± 0.06）、反刍动物以肾脏毒性为主（0.39 ± 0.07）、绵羊表现出皮肤敏感性（0.35 ± 0.07）；药物层面聚类与药理类别83%一致，余弦相似度表现最优（轮廓系数0.48，聚类准确率87%），且与现有监管分类高度吻合。

Conclusion: 监管融合的无监督分析可有效揭示具有生物学意义的区域特异性毒性模式，为兽药安全性评估提供可解释、可扩展的新框架。

Abstract: Veterinary pharmacovigilance systems are essential for monitoring adverse drug events (ADEs), yet existing approaches often fail to capture region-specific toxicity patterns shaped by local biological and regulatory contexts. In Japan, these challenges are amplified by species-specific metabolic differences and reporting practices defined by the Ministry of Agriculture, Forestry, and Fisheries (MAFF). Most prior work relies on prediction-oriented models, limiting mechanistic interpretability. This study proposes a regulatory-integrated unsupervised framework for pattern discovery using the National Veterinary Assay Laboratory (NVAL) database. ADEs are encoded into organ system-aligned representations and adjusted for species-specific reporting biases, enabling cross-species comparison. Similarity-based clustering and dimensionality reduction are applied to identify latent toxicity structures. Analysis of 4,120 high-confidence ADE reports (9,080 drug-ADE combinations) identified three significant species clusters (p < 0.01), including hepatic-dominant patterns in companion animals (0.42 $\pm$ 0.06), renal toxicity in ruminants (0.39 $\pm$ 0.07), and dermatological sensitivity in sheep (0.35 $\pm$ 0.07). Drug-level clustering achieved 83% alignment with pharmacological classes, while cosine similarity outperformed alternative metrics (silhouette score: 0.48; cluster precision: 87%). Regulatory validation showed strong agreement with established classifications. These findings demonstrate that regulation-aligned unsupervised analysis can uncover biologically meaningful, region-specific toxicity patterns, providing an interpretable and scalable framework for veterinary drug safety assessment.

</details>


### [227] [Evaluating Agentic Configuration Repair for Computer Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06212)
*Rufat Asadli,Benjamin Hoffman,Ioannis Protogeros,Laurent Vanbever*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在大型复杂网络场景中，尽管使用最先进的大语言模型（LLM）仍难以有效修复网络配置错误的问题，提出通过结合形式化验证和上下文检索工具的智能体架构来提升修复效果与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络中的配置错误是导致重大互联网中断的主要原因，而现有LLM在处理复杂网络配置修复时效率低且易引入新错误，因此需要更可靠的自动化解决方案。

Method: 对开源和闭源的大语言模型进行基准测试，结合形式化网络验证和上下文检索工具，采用具有动态上下文管理和迭代验证能力的智能体架构。

Result: 智能体架构相比基础LLM在修复有效性上平均提升12%，在安全性上平均提升17%。

Conclusion: 集成验证与上下文管理的智能体架构显著提升了大语言模型在网络配置修复中的性能与可靠性，为实际网络运维提供了更有前景的自动化方案。

Abstract: Misconfigurations in computer networks remain a major source of critical Internet outages. Research is turning to Large Language Models (LLMs) to automate the complex, error-prone task of network configuration. However, even state-of-the-art models fail to resolve misconfigurations in large-scale, complex scenarios and often introduce new errors. In this work, we benchmark open- and closed-source LLMs augmented with formal network verification and context retrieval tools. We demonstrate that agentic architectures outperform base LLMs in repair efficacy (by 12% on average) and safety (by 17% on average), enabled by the ability to dynamically manage context and iteratively validate configuration repairs.

</details>


### [228] [From Reward-Hack Activations to Agentic Risk States: Context-Calibrated Mechanistic Monitoring in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06223)
*Patrick Wilhelm,Odej Kao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究了基于激活的奖励黑客监控在语言模型代理中的应用，发现结合熵和决策上下文特征能更有效地识别风险行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高语言模型代理的安全性，需要有效监测其内部状态和环境背景下的奖励黑客行为。

Method: 使用激活基础的奖励黑客分数、令牌级别熵和决策上下文特征来监控ReAct风格代理在Gameable ALFWorld和WebShop环境中的行为，并通过微调适配器进行实验。

Result: 发现微调后的适配器可以将奖励黑客倾向转移到代理的动作选择中，特别是在环境暴露代理奖励机会时；但仅靠激活动态无法完全缓解这种行为；结合熵和上下文校准的内部特征可改善风险估计；激活方向引导进一步减少了特定混合适配器体制下的代理利用行为。

Conclusion: 支持上下文校准的内部监控方法：奖励黑客激活识别潜在策略状态，而熵和决策上下文有助于判断该状态何时变为危险动作。

Abstract: Language-model agents act through repeated cycles of observation, reasoning, and action selection, making safety monitoring depend on both internal model state and environment context. We study reward-hacking monitors in ReAct-style agents acting in Gameable ALFWorld and WebShop. Agents are instrumented with activation-based reward-hack scores, token-level entropy, and decision-context features. We find that adapters fine-tuned on \textit{School-of-Reward-Hacks} dataset can transfer reward-hack tendencies into agentic action selection, especially when the environment exposes proxy-reward affordances. However, mitigating such behavior cannot rely on activation dynamics alone. High reward-hack activation identifies a latent policy state, but does not necessarily imply an immediate exploit action. Across next-step prediction tasks, entropy and context-calibrated internal features improve risk estimation over reward-hack activation alone. Activation-direction steering further reduces proxy-exploit behavior in selected mixed-adapter regimes. Overall, our results support context-calibrated internal monitoring for agents: reward-hack activation identifies a latent policy state, while entropy and decision context help determine when that state becomes risky action.

</details>


### [229] [Closing the Loop on Latent Reasoning via Test-Time Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06252)
*Xiaopeng Yuan,Haibo Jin,Ye Yu,Peng Kuang,Lijun Yu,Yushun Dong,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReLAT是一种自监督的测试时训练方法，通过构建可微分的“问题->潜在思维->问题”循环，利用查询重构损失来优化潜在状态，从而在保持与原始查询一致性的同时提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的潜在推理方法由于将中间推理过程隐藏在不可见的表示中，导致无法检查中间状态是否仍然符合原始查询的约束，形成了开环推理，缺乏输入锚定的保真度验证。

Method: 提出ReLAT方法，构建一个可微分的双向循环：从问题生成潜在思维，再从中重建原始问题，并通过最小化重构损失来优化潜在状态。该过程在测试时进行自监督训练，确保潜在表示保留任务相关的信息。

Result: 在数学推理、知识问答和代码生成等多个基准上，ReLAT在Qwen系列模型中均优于单模型推断、基于文本的协作、开环潜在协作及其他测试时训练目标。在Qwen3-8B上，AIME 2024准确率从56.7%提升至73.3%，超越最强开环基线16.6个百分点。

Conclusion: ReLAT通过以原始查询为参考实现闭环潜在推理，有效增强了潜在状态与问题之间的语义一致性，显著提升了复杂任务下的推理性能。

Abstract: Recent work moves intermediate reasoning from natural-language traces into latent or cache-level representations to reduce token overhead and avoid a discrete communication bottleneck. However, this shift also removes a key advantage of textual reasoning: intermediate states are no longer inspectable, making it difficult to determine whether a latent state still preserves the constraints of the original query. As a result, latent reasoning typically operates in an open loop, where a latent state is produced and consumed without an input-anchored fidelity check. We propose ReLAT (Reconstruction-Guided Latent Reasoning At Test Time), a self-supervised test-time training method that closes this loop using the query itself as the reference. Our key observation is that if a latent state faithfully represents a query, the query should be recoverable from it; if the query cannot be recovered, the latent state has lost task-relevant information. ReLAT operationalizes this principle by constructing a differentiable Question -> Latent Thought -> Question cycle and optimizing query reconstruction loss through the latent thought before answer generation. This anchors opaque latent computation to the problem specification it is supposed to represent. Across mathematical reasoning, knowledge QA, and code generation benchmarks on the Qwen family, ReLAT consistently improves over single-model inference, text-based collaboration, open-loop latent collaboration, and alternative test-time training objectives. On Qwen3-8B, ReLAT raises AIME 2024 accuracy from 56.7% to 73.3%, a 16.6-point gain over the strongest open-loop latent baseline.

</details>


### [230] [RedKnot: Efficient Long-Context LLM Serving with Head-Aware KV Reuse and SegPagedAttention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06256)
*Yang Liu,ZhaoKai Luo,HuaYi Jin,ZhiYong Wang,RuoZhou He,BoYu Wang,Guanjie Chen,Junhao Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RedKnot提出了一种基于注意力头感知的KV缓存管理机制，通过在头级别分解KV缓存，提升大语言模型服务中的内存效率和可扩展性，支持多种优化策略而无需模型重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM服务系统采用单一的KV缓存抽象，导致资源利用效率低下；不同注意力头在功能角色、注意力距离和运行时重要性上存在显著差异，需更细粒度的管理机制。

Method: RedKnot将KV缓存按注意力头进行分解，构建头感知的结构化内存管理，实现位置无关的缓存复用、前缀压缩、冷热分离和分布式调度，统一支持多种优化场景。

Result: RedKnot在不牺牲输出质量的前提下，显著提升了GPU内存利用率、服务并发性和分布式可扩展性，有效支持多种KV缓存优化技术。

Conclusion: RedKnot为LLM服务提供了新的基础设施范式，将传统的单一块状KV缓存转变为动态、模型感知的运行时基底，推动高效可扩展的AI系统发展。

Abstract: As the input length of large language model (LLM) serving continues to grow, the KV cache has become a dominant bottleneck in AI infrastructure. It limits GPU memory capacity, serving concurrency, cache reuse, and distributed scalability. Several important problems, including position-independent KV cache, prefix KV cache compression, hot/cold KV cache separation, and distributed KV cache management, all depend on how the KV cache is represented and managed. However, existing serving systems largely rely on a monolithic KV cache abstraction, where the KV cache is treated as a homogeneous sequence of token-level memory blocks and managed with similar policies across attention heads and serving scenarios. We observe that KV cache utility is highly structured across KV heads: different heads exhibit different functional roles, attention distances, and runtime importance. Therefore, a full KV cache is not always necessary for every head, token range, or serving scenario.
  We present RedKnot, a head-aware KV cache management system for LLM serving. RedKnot breaks the conventional monolithic KV cache abstraction by decomposing the KV cache along KV heads, whose importance and effective attention ranges vary significantly across serving scenarios. This head-level decomposition turns the KV cache from a monolithic tensor abstraction into a structured memory object, enabling RedKnot to uniformly support position-independent KV reuse, prefix KV compression, hot/cold KV separation, and distributed KV placement while preserving output fidelity and improving resource efficiency, without requiring model retraining or fine-tuning. RedKnot establishes a new foundation for AI infrastructure by transforming the KV cache from a monolithic, passive runtime artifact into a dynamic, model-aware runtime substrate for scalable LLM serving.

</details>


### [231] [ToolChoiceConfusion: Causal Minimal Tool Filtering for Reliable LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06284)
*Rahul Suresh Babu,Laxmipriya Ganesh Iyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的因果最小工具过滤（CMTF）方法，通过轻量级前置条件-效果契约选择对当前步骤真正必要的工具，显著减少可见工具数量和令牌消耗，同时保持高任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具选择方法主要基于语义相关性，容易导致选择不必要或过早的工具，影响大模型代理在多步任务中的可靠性与效率。因此需要一种更精确的机制来判断工具是否为当前步骤所必需。

Method: CMTF利用轻量级的前置条件-效果契约建模工具的行为，仅暴露能从当前状态向目标推进的最小工具集合。该方法不依赖训练，而是基于因果充分性进行工具过滤，确保每一步只提供真正必要的工具。

Result: 在包含102个任务、100个工具和四种大模型的基准测试中，CMTF将每步可见工具从100个减少到平均1个，令牌使用量相对全工具暴露降低约90%，且整体任务成功率与最强的因果基线相当。

Conclusion: CMTF通过因果最小化原则有效提升了工具使用的效率与准确性，是一种高效、通用且适用于多种大模型的工具过滤框架。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly rely on external tools, but larger tool menus can reduce reliability and efficiency by increasing wrong-tool calls, premature actions, and token cost. Existing tool-selection methods often optimize semantic relevance, exposing tools whose names or descriptions match the user request. We argue that relevance is insufficient: a tool may be related to the task while still being unnecessary or premature at the current step.
  We propose Causal Minimal Tool Filtering (CMTF), a training-free method that selects tools by causal sufficiency. CMTF uses lightweight precondition-effect contracts to expose only the minimal next-step tool frontier needed to advance from the current state toward the user goal. Across multi-step tool-use tasks, we compare CMTF with all-tools exposure, keyword retrieval, state-aware filtering, and causal-path ablations, measuring task success, wrong-tool calls, premature actions, tool exposure, and token cost. In the main benchmark with 102 tasks, 100 tools, four LLM backends, and 2448 task-method-model runs, CMTF matches the strongest causal baseline in aggregate success while reducing visible tools from 100 to one per step and reducing token usage by about 90% relative to all-tools exposure.

</details>


### [232] [TRACE: A Temporal Conditional Estimation for Multimodal Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06285)
*Ziwen Kan,Yishuo Chen,Kecheng Li,Andrew Wen,Xiaomeng Wang,Liwei Wang,Jihao Duan,Song Wang,Hongfang Liu,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TRACE是一种针对多模态时间序列基础模型的条件估计范式，能够有效应对模态缺失和不规则采样问题，在医疗和情感计算等多个基准上表现出优于现有方法的鲁棒性和表示能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态时间序列处理方法在面对模态缺失和时间不对齐时通常采用简单的插补或掩码策略，忽略了跨模态依赖关系，导致表示质量下降。因此需要一种更有效的机制来处理现实场景中的不完整多模态数据。

Method: 提出TRACE，一种基于条件估计的多模态时间序列建模框架，通过可用的辅助模态信息对缺失的目标模态进行系统性推断，从而实现跨模态的表示学习与补全。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV、CMU-MOSI和CMU-MOSEI等多个真实多模态基准上的实验表明，TRACE在不同模态缺失设置下均优于先前方法，尤其在严重缺失情况下仍保持良好性能。

Conclusion: TRACE通过利用跨模态依赖关系显著提升了多模态时间序列模型在不完整数据下的鲁棒性和表示质量，为构建通用的时间序列基础模型提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TS-FMs) aim to learn generalizable temporal representations that can be adapted to a wide range of downstream tasks. In real-world multimodal settings, time series are frequently affected by temporal misalignment and partial modality missingness, where different modalities are observed at heterogeneous time scales or are partially absent. Existing approaches typically rely on naive imputation or masking strategies, which fail to account for cross-modal dependencies and often lead to misaligned or degraded representations. We propose TRACE, a conditional estimation paradigm for multimodal time series foundation model pipelines under missingness and irregular sampling, allowing incomplete target modalities to be systematically inferred from available auxiliary modalities. We evaluate TRACE on diverse multimodal benchmarks spanning healthcare and affective computing, including the MIMIC-IV clinical dataset and the CMU-MOSI and CMU-MOSEI benchmarks for multimodal sentiment analysis. Across a range of downstream prediction tasks and missing-modality settings, TRACE consistently outperforms prior multimodal fusion approaches, demonstrating improved robustness to severe modality missingness and more reliable cross-modal representations.

</details>


### [233] [Multi-ResNets for Subspace Preconditioning in Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06300)
*Merve Karakas,Christopher J. Williams,Emmanuel O. Balogun,Sadegh Sadeghi Tabas,Christian Brown,Nikhil Rao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MResOpt是一种用于约束优化问题的分阶段残差神经网络架构，通过优先级分解约束满足，并在预测-完成-修正流程中实现领域知识引导的有序约束处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法难以有效处理具有复杂约束结构的优化问题，尤其是当约束存在优先级顺序时。需要一种能够利用约束优先级信息并逐步满足约束的神经网络架构。

Method: 提出MResOpt架构，采用分阶段残差设计，在每一阶段引入中间重完成机制和阶段感知损失函数，结合领域知识对约束按优先级排序，并在理想无限宽度下分析其作为序列高斯过程回归的行为。

Result: 在合成的QP、QCQP和SOCP基准上，MResOpt在凸与非凸设置下均提升了高优先级约束的满足程度；在交流最优潮流问题中，相比重投影基线显著降低了高优先级约束违反，同时保持计算效率。

Conclusion: MResOpt通过阶段化设计和约束优先级建模，有效改善了复杂优化问题中关键约束的满足能力，支持物理启发的协作求解机制，适用于实际工程场景中的约束优化任务。

Abstract: We propose MResOpt, a staged residual neural network architecture for constrained optimization problems. Our architecture fits within predict-complete-correct pipelines and decomposes constraint satisfaction by priority through intermediate re-completion and stage-aware losses. The framework enables domain-informed ordered constraint satisfaction which allows the network to utilize ordinal structure when present. Under an idealized infinite-width regime, we show that our design behaves as sequential Gaussian Process regression. On synthetic QP, QCQP, and SOCP benchmarks, the staged architecture improves high-priority constraint satisfaction across convex and non-convex settings. On line-flow-constrained AC optimal power flow, we introduce a physics-motivated constraint ordering and show that MResOpt supports a learned division of labor that keeps iterates on the equality manifold, achieving substantially lower high-priority violation than reprojected baselines while remaining computationally efficient.

</details>


### [234] [AIS-Based Vessel Trajectory Prediction Using Memory-Augmented Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06311)
*Wonmo Koo,Sanha Chang,Heeyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了基于记忆的神经网络在船舶轨迹预测中的应用，利用AIS数据进行实验，结果表明其性能优于多种深度学习基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于现有方法在船舶轨迹预测方面存在不足，而记忆增强神经网络在其他移动对象预测中表现良好，因此探索其在 maritime 领域的应用具有重要意义。

Method: 采用记忆增强神经网络，利用外部记忆模块选择性地检索相关信息，并使用墨西哥湾和纽约湾的AIS数据进行实证研究。

Result: 实验结果显示，相比不包含外部记忆的深度学习基线模型，所提出的方法在船舶轨迹预测上表现出持续且显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 记忆增强机制能有效提升船舶轨迹预测精度，具备在海上安全与效率优化中应用的潜力。

Abstract: Accurate vessel trajectory prediction is essential for safe and efficient maritime operations, enabling collision avoidance and supporting route optimization. Although memory-augmented neural networks have recently shown strong performance in pedestrian and road-vehicle trajectory prediction by selectively retrieving relevant information from an external memory, their potential for vessel trajectory prediction remains underexplored. This paper presents an empirical investigation of memory-based trajectory prediction using Automatic Identification System (AIS) data. Experiments on data from the Gulf of Mexico and the New York Bight demonstrate consistent and substantial performance gains over a range of deep learning baselines that do not incorporate an external memory.

</details>


### [235] [LLM Self-Recognition: Steering and Retrieving Activation Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06315)
*Thibaud Ardoin,Jonas Schäfer,Gerhard Wunder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过操控大语言模型生成过程中的内部残差流来植入可检测指纹的方法，利用模型自身的表示结构实现对AI生成文本的可靠溯源，且不影响生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI生成内容的增多，需要一种可靠、高质量的溯源方法来区分不同大语言模型的输出，现有外部水印或检测技术存在局限性。

Method: 通过在生成过程中用随机稀疏向量引导模型的内部残差流，植入可识别的指纹信号，并使用另一LLM从激活中检测该信号以实现归因。

Result: 该方法在多种检测场景下达到超过98%的检测准确率，且不损害生成文本的质量。

Conclusion: 大语言模型具备可靠的自我识别能力，其激活空间具有可利用的结构特征，可用于无损嵌入可检测信号，为AI生成内容溯源提供了实用的新途径。

Abstract: Recent advances in interpretability suggest that large language models (LLMs) implicitly encode signals in their generated text that enable self-recognition of their outputs. We demonstrate that this capability is reliable, even in low-entropy scenarios, and that it can be amplified through targeted intervention. By steering the internal residual stream during generation with a random sparse vector, we create a detectable fingerprint that enables attribution of a given text to a specific LLM. This signal is recoverable from the activations of an LLM used as a detector, achieving over 98% accuracy across multiple detection settings while preserving the quality of generated text. As AI-generated content proliferates, this approach offers a practical alternative to traditional detectors by leveraging the model's natural representation structure for attribution rather than embedding a signal externally. Our contributions include: (i) establishing reliable self-recognition capabilities in LLMs, (ii) a simple steering mechanism enabling multi-LLM identification with no quality degradation, (iii) demonstrating that activation spaces contain exploitable structure for encoding signals without semantic interference.

</details>


### [236] [DragOn: A Benchmark and Dataset for Drag-Based GUI Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06322)
*Nathan Bout,Maxime Langevin,Ronan Riochet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DragOn，一个用于拖拽操作的基准测试和训练数据集，涵盖文本高亮、单元格选择、元素调整和滑块操作四个领域，旨在提升现有模型在复杂拖拽交互任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GUI代理在点击操作上已有较大进展，但由于缺乏大规模的拖拽标注数据，模型在复杂拖拽任务上表现不佳。因此需要构建一个大规模、多领域的拖拽接地数据集以推动该方向的发展。

Method: 作者构建了包含28.6万张截图和350万项训练任务的DragOn数据集，并设计了一个包含2000个样本的评测集，覆盖四种典型拖拽场景；在此基础上对多个闭源和开源视觉语言模型进行了评估，包括基于该数据集微调的Qwen VLM模型。

Result: 实验结果显示，使用DragOn数据集微调的Qwen VLM模型在拖拽任务上表现优于其他基线模型，表明该数据集有助于提升现有模型在下游计算机使用任务中的性能。

Conclusion: DragOn为拖拽操作提供了大规模训练与评估资源，有望成为GUI代理研究中的重要基准，推动视觉语言模型在复杂人机交互任务中的发展。

Abstract: GUI agents - vision-based models that control desktops, web browsers, and mobile devices through graphical user interfaces - promise to automate a wide range of digital tasks. While million-scale datasets have enabled substantial progress on click-grounding, drag grounding (e.g. drag-and-drop, swipe, highlight) data remains an order of magnitude smaller and current models fall short on complex drag-based interactions. We introduce DragOn, a drag grounding benchmark and training dataset covering four domains: text highlighting, cell selection, element resizing and slider manipulation. The dataset comprises 286K training screenshots and 3.5M training tasks, plus a 2000-example held-out evaluation suite. We evaluate proprietary (GPT, Claude) and open-weight (Qwen, Kimi, Holo) models, as well as a Qwen VLM fine-tuned on our training data. Results suggest that our dataset could improve performance of state-of-the-art models on downstream computer-use tasks.

</details>


### [237] [TokenMizer: Graph-Structured Session Memory for Long-Horizon LLM Context Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06337)
*Shweta Mishra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TokenMizer是一种基于类型化知识图的开源代理系统，用于解决大语言模型在长时任务中因上下文窗口有限而导致的历史信息丢失问题，通过结构化存储会话历史，在显著减少令牌消耗的同时提升决策与任务的可恢复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在处理长时任务时受限于最大有效上下文窗口，导致关键结构化信息被丢弃，且现有方法将历史视为平面文本，破坏了会话的可恢复性。

Method: 提出TokenMizer，将LLM会话历史建模为具有14种节点和7种边的类型化知识图，采用混合提取流水线增量构建图，并通过三级检查点机制生成紧凑的恢复块；设计八层压缩流水线降低上下文开销，并结合语义缓存减少查询延迟。

Result: 在涵盖5个领域的21个会话基准测试中，TokenMizer生成的恢复块平均仅78个token（基线为159-170），决策召回率高出9-17个百分点，任务回忆均值为51.0%，文件回忆为58.7%；消融实验显示模糊标签匹配是主要贡献因素，启发式压缩实现47.3%的令牌缩减。

Conclusion: TokenMizer以一半的令牌成本提供了可查询的、结构化的会话历史替代方案，优于传统的文本保留基线，特别适用于需要高保真度恢复的长期任务场景。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) deployments for long-horizon tasks face a fundamental constraint: context windows are finite while productive work sessions are not. When history exceeds the Maximum Effective Context Window (MECW), critical structured information - architectural decisions, task transitions, file histories - is silently discarded. Existing mitigations treat history as flat text, destroying the relational structure that makes sessions resumable. We present TokenMizer, an open-source proxy system that models LLM session history as a typed knowledge graph. The schema defines 14 node types and 7 edge types. A hybrid extraction pipeline populates the graph incrementally, while a three-tier checkpoint system serializes it into compact resume blocks. An 8-layer compression pipeline reduces context overhead, and a semantic cache reduces repeated-query latency. Evaluated on a controlled benchmark of 21 sessions spanning 5 domains, TokenMizer demonstrates significant token economy. It produces resume blocks averaging 78 tokens (range: 42-124) - 2x smaller than evaluated baselines (159-170 tokens) - while achieving higher decision recall (+9-17 percentage points). Crucially, baselines only preserve that a technology was mentioned; TokenMizer preserves the rationale. Across all sessions, TokenMizer achieves mean task recall 51.0%, decision recall 46.6%, and file recall 58.7%. Variance reflects domain heterogeneity: explicit imperative phrasing (software engineering) scores higher than implicit reasoning (research). Ablation studies show fuzzy label matching is the dominant improvement factor (+33 pp task recall). The heuristic compression achieves 47.3% token reduction with zero external dependencies. TokenMizer provides a queryable alternative to text-retention baselines at half the token cost.

</details>


### [238] [Boosting Brain-to-Image Decoding with TRIBE v2 Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06345)
*Yohann Benchetrit,Marlène Careil,Simon Dahan,Hubert Banville,Stéphane d'Ascoli,Jean-Rémi King*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了通过使用预训练模型生成的合成数据增强小规模fMRI数据集，以提升脑解码性能的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于标记神经数据的稀缺性，脑解码在低数据环境下受到限制，因此需要提高数据效率。

Method: 利用在超过1000小时的fMRI响应视频、音频和语言数据上预训练的TRIBE v2模型生成合成fMRI数据，并系统评估不同数量合成数据对图像解码器性能的影响。

Result: 在两个fMRI数据集上的实验显示，与仅使用真实数据训练的解码器相比，Top-10图像检索准确率最高提升了68%；某些情况下，仅使用合成fMRI数据训练的图像解码器也能表现优于随机水平。

Conclusion: 大规模fMRI响应模型可通过数据增强显著提升图像解码的数据效率，并支持零样本脑到图像解码。

Abstract: Brain decoding is limited by the availability of labeled neural data, and remains challenging in low-data regimes. To address this issue, we investigate whether and when brain decoding can be boosted by augmenting small fMRI datasets with synthetic data generated by a pretrained model of fMRI responses to stimuli. We use TRIBE v2, a large encoding model pretrained on more than 1000 hours of fMRI responses to video, audio and language. For each dataset, we evaluate systematic grids that show how the performance of image decoders varies with the amount of synthetic data used for training. Our results, based on two datasets (the 7T fMRI Natural Scenes Dataset and 3T fMRI BOLD5000), show up to 68% improvement in Top-10 image-retrieval accuracy compared to decoders trained only on real data. Importantly, the proportion of augmented data required to reach a given image decoding performance needs to be adjusted depending on the data source. Surprisingly, image decoders trained exclusively on synthetic fMRI can perform above chance in some settings, suggesting that TRIBE v2 can support zero-shot brain-to-image decoding. Together, these results show how large-scale models of the fMRI responses to sight, sound and language may provide a foundation to improve the data efficiency for image decoding.

</details>


### [239] [Where Should Knowledge Enter? A Layered Framework for Knowledge Infusion in Multimodal Iterative Generative Mo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06356)
*Renjith Prasad,Chathurangi Shyalika,Anushka Pawar,Amit Sheth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态生成模型中知识注入的四层干预框架，包括表面、轨迹、潜在和参数化层，并在扩散模型中验证了各层的互补性，显著减少了违反知识的输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态生成模型在需要遵循结构化或安全关键知识时仍不可靠，且现有方法缺乏对生成过程中不同组件进行系统性知识注入的统一框架。

Method: 将生成过程视为内部状态的演变轨迹，提出四类干预层（表面、轨迹、潜在、参数），并在扩散模型中实现前三层的组合，通过控制实验评估其效果。

Result: 在两个扩散模型骨干上使用多模态知识图谱进行实验，逐步叠加表面层（输入/输出端）和轨迹-潜在层（生成中期），相比基础生成方式减少70.97%的知识违反输出。

Conclusion: 知识注入应基于生成过程的结构组件进行分层设计，不同干预层具有互补性，多层组合能更有效地确保生成内容符合领域或安全知识。

Abstract: Multimodal generative models produce fluent outputs but remain unreliable when generation must respect structured, domain-specific, or safety-critical knowledge. Existing methods incorporate knowledge through mechanisms such as prompt augmentation, guidance, latent editing, or fine-tuning, yet they are typically categorized by technique rather than by the component of the generative process they modify. We argue that knowledge infusion in iterative generative models is fundamentally anintervention-layer problem. Since thegenerative process unfolds as a trajectory of internal states, knowledge can act on four structurally distinct components of this process: the input/output boundary, the transition function, the intermediate state, and the model parameters. This maps to four intervention layers: surface, trajectory, latent, and parametric infusion. We instantiate the framework in diffusion models, map representative methods to all four layers, and derive design principles for multi-layer composition. In a controlled safety-alignment experiment using a multimodal knowledge graph with two diffusion backbones, we implement three of the four layers cumulatively, surface (input-side and output-side) and trajectory--latent (mid-generation). We show empirically that each additional layer addresses failure classes that prior layers cannot reach, reducing knowledge-violating outputs by 70.97% compared to vanilla generation and empirically confirming the framework's complementarity prediction.

</details>


### [240] [An Infectious Disease Spread Simulation Based on Large Language Model Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06360)
*Yonchanok Khaokaew,Ruochen Kong,Andreas Zufle,Hao Xue,Taylor Anderson,Chandini Raina MacIntyre,Matthew Scotch,Flora D. Salim,David J Heslop*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合大语言模型与基于人口普查的合成人群的空间代理模拟框架，用于模拟流感样疾病自我报告行为，揭示收入和教育是报告率差异的主要驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更好地理解传染病暴发期间个体决策的行为动态，并支持具有地理和社会异质性的公共卫生干预建模。

Method: 构建一个空间定位的基于代理的模拟框架，将大语言模型生成的决策整合到基于人口普查数据的合成人群中，考虑位置、家庭影响和信息框架三种情景，在旧金山和亚特兰大进行模拟。

Result: 收入和教育水平是影响疾病自我报告率的主要因素，地理位置、大语言模型选择和信息框架也有较小但一致的影响。

Conclusion: 该框架能够生成包含社会与地理异质性的合成行为数据，有助于空间流行病学建模和偏差感知的行为分析。

Abstract: Modelling individual decision-making during infectious disease outbreaks is crucial for understanding behavioural dynamics and informing effective public health interventions. Prior work has shown that large language models can simulate realistic human behaviour by generating agent decisions based on demographic prompts and situational context. We build on this foundation with a spatially grounded, agent-based simulation framework that integrates LLM-generated decisions about self-reported influenza-like illness into a census-based synthetic population of agents. Location is treated as a central feature: agents are assigned to spatial units within cities, capturing the spatial distributions of different demographic groups using real-world census data and enabling geographically diverse behavioural modelling. We implement and compare three decision scenarios, independent reasoning, household influence, and message framing, and simulate self-reporting outcomes in San Francisco and Atlanta. Results reveal that income and education are the dominant drivers of reporting rate variation, with smaller but consistent effects from geography, LLM model choice, and message framing. Our framework generates synthetic data that captures both social and geographic heterogeneity, supporting spatial epidemiological modelling and bias-aware behavioural analysis.

</details>


### [241] [Rethinking Infrastructure Inspection as Image Difference Classification: A Traffic Sign Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06375)
*Ching Yau Fergus Mok,Lavindra de Silva,Varun Kumar Reja,Ioannis Brilakis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图像差异分类（IDC）的方法，用于减少道路基础设施检测中数字孪生技术对标注数据的依赖，特别适用于低资源条件下的交通标志检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于标注数据有限，数字孪生在道路基础设施检测中的应用受到限制，本文旨在通过利用资产状态监测的关联性来降低数据依赖。

Method: 将基于图像的缺陷检测重新定义为图像差异分类（IDC），并使用新构建的高质量数据集评估了多种IDC分类器在交通标志检测中的表现。

Result: 基于指令的分类器优于基于编码器的分类器，并能通过与参考图像比较提升性能。

Conclusion: IDC是一种有效的任务建模方法，有助于缓解基础设施检测和数字孪生资产状态更新中的数据约束问题。

Abstract: Digital twins (DTs) allow the digitalization of road infrastructure inspection, though this is hindered by limited annotated data. This work exploits the relational nature of continuous asset condition monitoring to reformulate image-based defect detection as image difference classification (IDC) to reduce data reliance. This was evaluated in a case study on low-resource traffic sign inspection with different IDC classifiers using a newly-curated, high quality dataset. Results indicate that the instruction-based classifier outperforms encoder-based ones and gains from comparison with reference images. This shows that IDC can be an effective task modeling for tackling data constraints in infrastructure inspection and DT asset condition updating.

</details>


### [242] [Humans' ALMANAC: A Human Collaboration Dataset of Action-Level Mental Model Annotations for Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06388)
*Jiaju Chen,Yuxuan Lu,Jiayi Su,Chaoran Chen,Songlin Xiao,Zheng Zhang,Yun Wang,Yunyao Li,Jian Zhao,Tongshuang Wu,Toby Jia-Jun Li,Dakuo Wang,Bingsheng Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ALMANAC数据集，用于提升大语言模型代理在人类协作中的心理模型对齐能力，包含2987个协作行为及相应的心理模型标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的LLM代理主要优化任务完成，缺乏在协作过程中持续维护和对齐心理模型的能力，且缺少带有细粒度心理模型标注的真实人类协作数据。

Method: 基于社会科学研究中的经典双人导航任务（Map Task）构建ALMANAC数据集，包含动作级别的心理模型标注，涵盖自我推理、对伙伴意图的理解和团队目标认知，并对六种LLM进行基准测试。

Result: ALMANAC数据集有效支持对模型预测人类下一轮行为和心理模型能力的评估，展示了其在模拟人类协作行为方面的潜力。

Conclusion: ALMANAC为训练和评估具备过程级协作能力的智能代理提供了重要资源，推动LLM代理向更真实的人类协作模式发展。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLM agents have enabled complex cognitive capabilities, such as multi-step reasoning, planning, and tool use, that increasingly position these agents as human collaborators. Effective collaboration, however, requires collaborators to continuously maintain and align mental models of their own reasoning,partners' intentions, and shared goals during the collaborative process. Today's agents rarely develop such capabilities since they are primarily optimized for task completion, and the community lacks authentic human collaboration data with action-level mental model annotations that could guide agents toward process-level collaborative competence. To bridge this gap, we present ALMANAC, a dataset of Action-Level Mental model ANnotations for Agent Collaboration built from the Map Task, a classic dyadic routing task from social science. ALMANAC contains 2,987 collaboration actions, each paired with theory-informed mental model annotations that record the participants' self-reasoning, perceived partner intent, and perceived team goal. We benchmark six LLMs on predicting humans' next-turn behavior and mental models. Our results demonstrate ALMANAC's utility in evaluating models' ability to simulate human collaborative behaviors and infer their underlying mental models.

</details>


### [243] [Risk Assessment of Autonomous Driving: Integrating Technical Failures, Ethical Dilemmas, and Policy Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06396)
*Boyi Chen,Shengqin Chu,Zicheng Wang,Brian Baetz,Zhen Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了自动驾驶技术在减少人为错误导致的交通事故方面的潜力，同时分析了其面临的技术、伦理和监管风险，提出需要通过工程标准、伦理讨论和制度监督相结合的适应性协同治理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶技术虽然有望降低交通事故率，但其带来的新型风险（如技术故障、伦理决策和法规不一致）尚未得到系统评估，亟需跨领域研究以推动其安全应用。

Method: 基于NHTSA的公开事故数据、加州DMV的脱离报告、MIT Moral Machines数据集以及五个司法管辖区的比较监管分析，识别主要技术失效模式并探讨伦理与监管差异。

Result: 发现感知与分类错误是主要技术故障，占报告事故的较大比例；不同地区存在不同的伦理决策框架和不一致的法规，增加了广泛应用的不确定性。

Conclusion: 技术和伦理问题相互关联，必须与监管协同解决，建议采用结合工程标准、伦理讨论和制度监督的适应性协同治理方式。

Abstract: Autonomous driving technology has the potential to reduce the large number of road traffic accidents caused by human error each year, but it also brings new types of risks that need to be evaluated from the aspects of technology, ethics and regulations. Based on public crash data from the National Highway Traffic Safety Administration (NHTSA), disengagement reports from the California Department of Motor Vehicles (DMV), the MIT Moral Machines dataset, and a comparative regulatory analysis of five jurisdictions, we have found that the main types of technical failure modes are perception and classification errors. These account for a relatively large proportion of the reported accidents, and it can be concluded that there are different ethical frameworks for autonomous vehicle decision-making, and inconsistent regulations in different areas increase the uncertainty of widespread application. Generally speaking, the problems of technology, ethics and regulation are closely related and need to be solved together. Therefore, this paper recommends a more adaptive and cooperative governance approach that combines engineering standards, ethical discussion, and institutional supervision.

</details>


### [244] [Unsupervised Skill Discovery for Agentic Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06416)
*Zhisong Qiu,Kangqi Song,Shengwei Tang,Shuofei Qiao,Lei Liang,Huajun Chen,Shumin Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DataCOPE的无监督技能发现框架，用于提升数据分析智能体在推理时的表现，无需更新模型参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据分析技能获取依赖昂贵的监督信号，且不同分析任务的成功标准不一，难以有效发现可复用的技能。

Method: DataCOPE通过探索轨迹生成验证信号，协调数据分析智能体、无监督验证器和技能管理器，进行对比式技能提炼。针对报告型分析采用自适应检查表验证器，针对推理型分析采用答案一致性验证器。

Result: 在Deep Data Research和DABStep两个任务上验证，DataCOPE在报告型和推理型任务中平均得分分别提升9.71%和32.30%，显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: DataCOPE能够从无标签的探索中有效发现可复用的数据分析技能，显著提升智能体在多种分析任务上的泛化性能。

Abstract: Inference-time skill augmentation provides a lightweight way to improve data-analytic agents by injecting reusable procedural knowledge without updating model parameters. However, discovering effective skills for data analysis remains challenging, as reliable supervision is expensive and success criteria vary across analytical formats. This raises the key question of how to discover reusable data-analysis skills from unlabeled exploration alone. We propose DataCOPE, an unsupervised verifier-guided skill discovery framework for data-analytic agents. DataCOPE derives verifier signals from the exploration trajectories and uses them to characterize relative quality or aggreement among trajectories. It iteratively coordinates a Data-Analytic Agent for trajectory generation, an Unsupervised Verifier for signal extraction, and a Skill Manager for contrastive skill distillation. For report-style analysis, we instantiate the verifier as an Adaptive Checklist Verifier that derives task-specific criteria, scores reports by verifiable coverage, and iteratively refines the checklist. For reasoning-style analysis, we instantiate it as an Answer Agreement Verifier that groups trajectories by answer agreement and uses self-consistency as an auxiliary signal. We evaluate DataCOPE on report-style analysis from Deep Data Research and reasoning-style analysis from DABStep. Across both settings, DataCOPE consistently improves held-out performance over baselines. Averaged across four model settings, DataCOPE improves the mean score by 9.71% and 32.30% on report-style and reasoning-style tasks respectively.

</details>


### [245] [Agent Memory: Characterization and System Implications of Stateful Long-Horizon Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06448)
*Yasmine Omri,Ziyu Gan,Zachary Broveak,Robin Geens,Zexue He,Alex Pentland,Marian Verhelst,Tsachy Weissman,Thierry Tambe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次对LLM智能体的记忆系统进行了系统性表征，提出了分类法、评测框架，并通过实验分析揭示了不同设计选择对读写开销的影响，最终给出了10条系统优化建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体被广泛应用于需要长期记忆和持续推理的复杂任务，现有多种记忆系统缺乏统一的系统级评估，难以理解其在实际部署中的行为特征与成本分布。

Method: 提出一个面向系统的四维分类法；构建支持阶段感知的评测框架，将开销分解为构建、检索和生成三个阶段；在两个基准套件上对十种代表性系统进行实证分析。

Result: 揭示了不同记忆系统设计如何影响读写路径的成本分布，明确了各类方法在延迟、新鲜度、查询吞吐等方面的权衡；验证了系统层面的关键瓶颈与优化机会。

Conclusion: 为LLM智能体记忆系统的设计与部署提供了实证基础和系统指导，提出的10条建议有助于在大规模应用中实现高效、可管理的记忆架构。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly deployed on long-horizon tasks requiring sustained reasoning over extended interaction histories. Realizing this at scale requires agents to persistently store, retrieve, and update their own memory across sessions. A rich ecosystem of agent memory systems has emerged spanning flat retrieval, LLM-mediated extraction, consolidating fact stores, and agentic control flows. Yet, their system-level behavior remains uncharacterized. We present the first systems characterization of agent memory. First, we introduce a system-oriented taxonomy classifying agent memory systems along four axes. Second, we build a phase-aware profiling harness attributing cost to construction, retrieval, and generation. Third, we characterize ten representative systems across two benchmark suites, uncovering how design choices shift cost across the write and read paths. Finally, we derive 10 system recommendations covering construction scheduling, capability floors, amortization via query volume, freshness-latency tradeoffs, and fleet-scale management.

</details>


### [246] [Vortex: Efficient and Programmable Sparse Attention Serving for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06453)
*Zhuoming Chen,Xinrui Zhong,Qilong Feng,Ranajoy Sadhukhan,Yang Zhou,Michael Qizhe Shieh,Zhihao Jia,Beidi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Vortex是一个结合了Python嵌入式前端语言和页中心张量抽象的系统，用于高效表达、部署和评估稀疏注意力算法，显著提升了大语言模型推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的稀疏注意力算法在大规模部署和评估时工程复杂度高，限制了研究人员和AI代理对稀疏注意力设计的探索速度。

Method: 提出Vortex系统，采用Python嵌入式前端语言和页中心张量抽象来表达多种稀疏注意力算法，并与现代LLM服务堆栈紧密集成的后端实现高效执行。

Result: AI代理利用Vortex自动生成并优化稀疏注意力算法，在保持准确性的前提下最高实现3.46倍于全注意力的吞吐量；在MLA-based GLM-4.7-Flash上达到4.7倍吞吐提升，在229B参数的MiniMax-M2.7模型上实现1.37倍提升。

Conclusion: Vortex大幅加速了稀疏注意力算法的设计与迭代过程，支持在新兴架构和超大规模模型上的快速实验，有效将理论效率转化为实际性能增益。

Abstract: Sparse attention is becoming increasingly important for serving large language models (LLMs) as generation lengths continue to grow. However, deploying and evaluating new sparse attention algorithms at scale remains highly engineering-intensive, slowing both human researchers and AI agents in exploring the sparse attention design. To address this challenge, we present Vortex, a system that combines a Python-embedded frontend language atop a page-centric tensor abstraction for expressing a broad range of sparse attention algorithms, with an efficient backend tightly integrated into modern LLM serving stacks. Vortex enables rapid prototyping, deployment, and evaluation of sparse attention algorithms, effectively translating their theoretical efficiency gains into real-world throughput improvements. As a result, Vortex substantially accelerates the design and iteration of sparse attention algorithms. First, AI agents use Vortex to automatically generate and refine diverse algorithms, the best reaching up to $3.46\times$ higher throughput than full attention while preserving accuracy. Second, Vortex extends sparse attention to emerging architectures and very large models that are otherwise hard to experiment with, reaching up to $4.7\times$ higher throughput on the MLA-based GLM-4.7-Flash and $1.37\times$ on the 229B-parameter MiniMax-M2.7 on NVIDIA B200 GPUs.

</details>


### [247] [Benchmark Everything Everywhere All at Once](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06462)
*Shiyun Xiong,Dongming Wu,Peiwen Sun,Yuang Ai,Bokang Yang,Wencheng Han,Xiao-Hui Li,Xiangyu Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Benchmark Agent的全自动代理系统，用于自动生成高质量、可持续的基准测试集，覆盖文本理解、多模态理解和领域特定推理等场景，显著减少人工参与，并揭示当前模型在某些任务上的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试构建耗时耗力、复用性差且易被先进模型迅速饱和，缺乏持续有效的评估能力，因此需要一种自动化、可扩展的基准生成方案。

Method: 设计并实现了一个端到端的自主代理系统Benchmark Agent，能够自动完成从用户需求分析、子任务设计到数据标注和质量控制的完整基准构建流程。

Result: 成功生成15个代表性基准测试，实验表明所生成样本质量高，具备良好区分度，并发现当前模型在特定领域推理任务中表现不佳。

Conclusion: Benchmark Agent能高效、自动地构建多样化且具有挑战性的基准测试，有助于推动LLM和MLLM的持续评估与发展。

Abstract: Benchmarks are fundamental for evaluating and advancing LLMs and MLLMs by providing standardized and explicit measures of performance. However, their construction is labor-intensive and hard to reuse, raising concerns about sustainability and scalability. Moreover, existing benchmarks often quickly reach performance saturation after their release, resulting in insufficient discrimination among state-of-the-art models. To address these challenges, we introduce Benchmark Agent, a fully autonomous agentic system designed for benchmark building. Our framework orchestrates the complete benchmark construction pipeline, from user query analysis and subtask design to data annotation and quality control. To assess Benchmark Agent, we implement it to produce 15 representative benchmarks, spanning diverse evaluation scenarios, including text understanding, multimodal understanding, and domain-specific reasoning. Extensive experiments, including human evaluation, LLM-as-a-judge assessment, and consistency checks, demonstrate Benchmark Agent can generate high-quality benchmark samples with minimal human involvement. More importantly, through continual evaluation, we observe several insightful findings, including that current models struggle with certain domain-specific reasoning tasks. We believe that rapidly evolving benchmarks can contribute significantly to the research community. The preview and code will be publicly available at the demo page and code repository.

</details>


### [248] [Goedel-Architect: Streamlining Formal Theorem Proving with Blueprint Generation and Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06468)
*Jui-Hui Chung,Ziyang Cai,Zihao Li,Qishuo Yin,Rohit Agarwal,Simon Park,Rodrigo Porto,Narutatsu Ri,Ziran Yang,Shange Tang,Xingyu Dang,Hongzhou Lin,Mengdi Wang,Danqi Chen,Chi Jin,Liam H Fowl,Sanjeev Arora*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Goedel-Architect 是一个基于蓝图生成与优化的代理框架，用于在 Lean 4 中进行形式化定理证明，实现了当前开源系统中最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统递归引理分解方法容易陷入无效策略循环，效率低下，需要一种更高效的全局规划机制来提升形式化定理证明的成功率。

Method: 提出 Goedel-Architect 框架，首先生成包含定义和引理依赖关系的形式化蓝图，可由自然语言证明引导；然后使用工具增强的 Lean 证明器并行闭合各引理节点，并根据失败反馈迭代优化全局蓝图。

Result: 在 MiniF2F-test 上达到 99.2% pass@1，PutnamBench 上达 75.6% pass@1；引入自然语言证明后进一步提升至 MiniF2F-test 100%、PutnamBench 88.8%，并在 IMO 2025、Putnam 2025 和 USAMO 2026 等难题上取得显著成果。

Conclusion: Goedel-Architect 通过蓝图驱动的并行证明与反馈优化机制，在性能上显著超越现有主流方法，是目前性价比最高（成本低至500倍）的开源定理证明框架之一。

Abstract: We introduce Goedel-Architect, an agentic framework for formal theorem proving in Lean 4 centered on blueprint generation and refinement. A blueprint is a dependency graph of definitions and lemmas that builds up to the main theorem. First, Goedel-Architect generates a blueprint of formally stated definitions and lemmas, along with declared dependencies. This blueprint is optionally guided by a natural language proof. Then, a tool-equipped Lean prover component closes each open lemma node in parallel using relevant dependencies. Failed lemmas in turn drive refinement of the global blueprint. This strategy contrasts with other mainstream approaches which use recursive lemma decomposition, and can inefficiently loop on dead-end strategies. Using the open-weight DeepSeek-V4-Flash (284B-A13B) as the backbone, Goedel-Architect attains 99.2% pass@1 on MiniF2F-test and 75.6% pass@1 on PutnamBench. With an optional natural-language proof seeding the initial blueprint on the harder problems, we additionally close the remaining two MiniF2F-test problems (reaching 100%), lift PutnamBench to 88.8% (597/672), and solve 4/6 on IMO 2025, 11/12 on Putnam 2025, and 3/6 on USAMO 2026. This represents state-of-the-art performance for an open-source pipeline at a price point up to 500x less than comparable open-source pipelines.

</details>


### [249] [MLEvolve: A Self-Evolving Framework for Automated Machine Learning Algorithm Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06473)
*Shangheng Du,Xiangchao Yan,Jinxin Shi,Zongsheng Cao,Shiyang Feng,Zichen Liang,Boyuan Sun,Tianshuo Peng,Yifan Zhou,Xin Li,Jie Zhou,Liang He,Bo Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MLEvolve 是一个基于大语言模型的自进化多智能体框架，通过改进的树搜索、跨分支信息共享、回溯记忆机制和分层控制，实现高效的端到端机器学习算法发现，在多个基准上达到领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习工程（MLE）智能体在长周期任务中存在跨分支信息隔离、无记忆搜索和缺乏分层控制的问题，限制了其持续自我进化的能力。

Method: 提出 MLEvolve 框架，扩展树搜索为 Progressive MCGS，引入图结构参考边实现跨分支信息流动；设计基于熵的渐进调度策略，平衡探索与利用；构建 Retrospective Memory，结合先验知识库与动态全局记忆以支持经验复用；并通过解耦战略规划与代码生成实现自适应编码模式。

Result: 在 MLE-Bench 上，MLEvolve 在12小时预算内（仅为标准时长一半）实现了最先进的平均奖牌率和有效提交率，并在数学算法优化任务上超越 AlphaEvolve 等专用方法，展现出强跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: MLEvolve 通过结构化记忆、信息共享和分层控制机制，显著提升了 LLM 智能体在长周期、复杂优化任务中的持续进化能力，推动了自主算法发现的发展。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly applied to long-horizon tasks such as scientific discovery and machine learning engineering (MLE), where sustained self-evolution becomes a key capability. However, existing MLE agents suffer from inter-branch information isolation, memoryless search, and lack of hierarchical control, which together hinder long-horizon optimization. We present MLEvolve, an LLM-based self-evolving multi-agent framework for end-to-end machine learning algorithm discovery. By extending tree search to Progressive MCGS, MLEvolve enables cross-branch information flow through graph-based reference edges and gradually shifts the search from broad exploration to focused exploitation with an entropy-inspired progressive schedule. To allow the agent to evolve with accumulated experience, we introduce Retrospective Memory, which combines a cold-start domain knowledge base with a dynamic global memory for task-specific experience retrieval and reuse. For stable long-horizon iteration, we further decouple strategic planning from code generation with adaptive coding modes. Evaluation on MLE-Bench shows that MLEvolve achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple dimensions including average medal rate and valid submission rate under a 12-hour budget (half the standard runtime). Moreover, MLEvolve also outperforms specialized algorithm discovery methods including AlphaEvolve on mathematical algorithm optimization tasks, demonstrating strong cross-domain generalization. Our code is available at https://github.com/InternScience/MLEvolve.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [250] [OLIVE: Online Low-Rank Incremental Learning for Efficient Adaptive Exoskeletons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05234)
*Dong Liu,Yanxuan Yu,Ben Lengerich,Tony Geng,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出OLIVE，一种参数高效的在线自适应框架，用于个性化外骨骼控制，通过低秩增量学习和传感器反馈实现动态环境下的实时适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有外骨骼控制器依赖静态步态策略，难以适应动态真实环境和个体差异，缺乏个性化能力。

Method: 采用低秩残差形式分解控制策略的自适应部分，利用EMG、IMU等体上传感器反馈驱动奖励塑造的策略梯度进行在线更新，并引入门控机制和动态秩调度器根据地形复杂度调整更新维度。

Result: 在约1800步内收敛，端到端延迟仅7.4ms，相比最强基线在步态平滑性、 effort reduction 和运动稳定性上分别提升13%、22%和15%。

Conclusion: OLIVE实现了高效、稳定的在线个性化外骨骼控制，适用于多种地形场景，具备良好的实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Wearable exoskeleton systems hold promise for restoring mobility in individuals with physical impairments, yet most existing controllers rely on static gait policies that lack the ability to adapt to dynamic real-world environments or individual user characteristics. We present \olive (\underline{O}nline \underline{L}ow-rank \underline{I}ncremental Learning for Efficient Adapti\underline{ve} Exoskeletons), a parameter-efficient online adaptation framework that continuously personalizes exoskeleton control during deployment. \olive decomposes the adaptive component of the control policy into a low-rank residual form~$\dW = \At\Bt^\top$ with rank~$r!\ll!\min(d,k)$, reducing online update cost from $\mathcal{O}(dk)$ to $\mathcal{O}(r(d{+}k))$ while preserving the stability of a pretrained base controller~$\Wz$. Parameters are updated via a reward-shaped policy gradient driven purely by on-body sensor feedback (EMG, IMU, vibration), eliminating dependence on offline reference trajectories. A gating mechanism modulates the strength of personalization based on contextual state, and a dynamic rank scheduler adapts the update dimensionality to terrain complexity -- allocating minimal capacity on simple flat terrain and expanding to higher-rank updates on demanding uneven surfaces -- enabling robust performance across diverse activities: flat walking, stair navigation, slopes, and uneven terrain. Experiments on the wearable platform demonstrate that \olive achieves +13, +22, and +15 percentage-point improvements in gait smoothness, effort reduction, and motion stability over the strongest baseline, converging within $\sim$1{,}800 walking steps at 7.4,ms end-to-end latency. Our code implementation is available at https://github.com/FastLM/OLIVE.

</details>


### [251] [A New Quaternion-Joint Cable-Driven Redundant Manipulator Configuration and its Control Through FABRIK and Residual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05236)
*Tanapath Pornthisan,Thanapat Kemthong,Thanyapisit Kangsathien,Pasut Aranchaiya,Paulo Garcia,Viboon Sangveraphunsiri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于四元数关节的4段8关节电缆驱动冗余机械臂，结合残差强化学习实现高精度控制，相较于传统的FABRIK算法在位置和姿态精度上提升三个数量级，且硬件成本更低、工作空间更广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电缆驱动冗余机械臂因运动学模型复杂、计算负担重及制造误差影响导致控制困难，需更高效的设计与控制方法以拓展其在狭窄复杂环境中的应用。

Method: 设计了一种新型4段8关节的四元数关节机械臂结构，并采用残差强化学习进行控制，同时对比了FABRIK算法的性能，给出了完整的控制与学习实现流程。

Result: 该机械臂具有更广的工作空间和更高的控制精度，残差强化学习在位置和姿态误差上比FABRIK算法提高三个数量级，控制系统实现更简单，硬件成本更低。

Conclusion: 四元数关节结合残差强化学习为电缆驱动冗余机械臂提供了更优的设计与控制方案，显著提升了性能与实用性，适用于未来复杂场景下的机器人系统开发。

Abstract: Robotic arms capable of traversing arbitrary spatial paths, especially in highly obstructed workspaces, are highly desired across several industries. Quaternion-joints have recently empowered a specific class of robotic arms -- cable-driven redundant manipulators -- beyond its prior capabilities. Specifically, quaternion-joints reduce the number of required motors per degree of freedom, paving the way for more compact solutions.An ongoing challenge is that the complexity of the kinematic model of quaternion joints challenges a priori decisions on manipulator configurations and imposes higher computational demands on the control system and its non-linearities amplify all discrepancies between design and physical artifact arising from fabrication imprecision. Here we show a that a 4-segment, 8-joint manipulator can achieve a broader workspace than extant configurations, at lower hardware cost, and that Residual Reinforcement Learning outperforms extant state-of-the-art methods -- specifically, the FABRIK algorithm -- on the control of such manipulator. Our results show that this configuration is more workspace-effective than prior designs, and that Residual Reinforcement Learning outperforms FABRIK by three orders of magnitude on positional and orientational accuracy, effecting precise control of the novel 4-segment, 8-joint manipulator. Additionally, the control implementation is simpler: we describe the complete FABRIK process for control and corresponding learning implementation. Our methodology is applicable to the design of new systems, providing designers with further tools for the development of this class of manipulators and corresponding control systems for novel configurations.

</details>


### [252] [Inverse Manipulation through Symbolic Planning and Residual Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05248)
*Yigit Yildirim,Giuseppe Rauso,Riccardo Caccavale,Alberto Finzi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合框架，通过从演示中提取的软几何谓词自动生成STRIPS-like算子，结合任务规划与强化学习，实现机器人操作技能的逆向执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的符号逆向规划在连续交互动态下无法完全恢复正向操作的效果，需要一种能够结合符号规划与物理交互的逆向操作方法。

Method: 从演示中提取带有软几何谓词的STRIPS-like算子，为每个算子构建逆向恢复目标（保持前提条件、恢复删除效果、否定添加效果）；先用任务规划器尝试满足目标，未解决的谓词则转化为残差操作学习问题，使用强化学习（如SAC）进行优化。

Result: 在ManiSkill3 PushCube任务中验证，前向推力技能的符号逆向仅能粗略恢复物体位置，而基于残差的SAC策略可进一步精调立方体姿态，满足剩余逆向谓词。

Conclusion: 基于谓词导出的残差控制能将近似的符号逆向转化为物理上可行的精确逆向技能，有效结合了符号推理与持续控制的优势。

Abstract: Inverting a robotic task requires more than reversing symbolic state transitions or rewinding motor trajectories. In robot manipulation tasks, symbolic inverse plans often fail to fully restore the effects of forward executions under continuous interaction dynamics. We present a hybrid framework for inverse manipulation that derives inverse-skill objectives from STRIPS-like operators automatically extracted from demonstrations through soft geometric predicates. For each extracted operator, we construct an inverse restoration objective that preserves preconditions, restores delete effects, and negates add effects. A task planner first attempts to satisfy this objective using available action primitives. Unresolved symbolic predicates then induce a residual operator learning problem solved through Reinforcement Learning (RL). We evaluate the framework on the ManiSkill3 PushCube task. For a forward pushing skill, the symbolic inverse performs a coarse pick-and-place restoration, while a residual Soft Actor-Critic policy refines the cube pose to satisfy the remaining inverse predicates. Our results show that predicate-derived residual control can turn an approximate symbolic inverse into a physically grounded inverse skill.

</details>


### [253] [Efficient Computation of Distance Functions for Navigation Vector Fields in Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05372)
*Vinicius M. Gonçalves,João Baião,Felipe Bartelt,Douglas G. Macharet,Gustavo M. Freitas,Héctor Azpúrua,Luciano C. A. Pimenta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效计算点与G-多项式曲线（推广到李群的多项式曲线）之间距离的方法，利用曲线结构将问题简化为少量多项式求根运算，显著降低了计算时间并保持精度，适用于高频率控制场景，并在SE(3)群和机器人 manipulator 上进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于向量场的机器人控制中，频繁计算机器人位形与路径曲线之间的距离及最近点可能非常耗时，尤其是在李群上实现时，这对嵌入式平台上的实时控制构成了挑战。因此需要一种更高效的计算方法。

Method: 提出了一种针对G-多项式曲线的高效距离计算方法，该方法利用G-多项式曲线在李群上的结构特性，将距离计算问题转化为少数几个多项式方程的求根问题，从而减少计算开销。同时提供了SE(3)群下的实用计算公式。

Result: 仿真结果表明，与现有的基于优化的方法相比，所提方法显著减少了计算时间，同时保持了较高的精度；并通过在机械臂上的实验验证了其有效性。此外，该方法已实现在一个在线可获取的计算软件包中。

Conclusion: 该方法通过利用G-多项式曲线的结构特性，有效提升了在李群上进行路径跟踪时的距离计算效率，适合应用于实时性要求高的嵌入式机器人控制系统。

Abstract: Vector-field-based methods are widely used for robot control and are often applied to the path-tracking problem. Some vector field approaches require repeatedly computing the distance between the robot configuration and the curve, as well as the corresponding closest point. Recently, vector fields have been extended to Lie Groups. In this case, this computation can be expensive, especially when performed at high control frequencies on embedded platforms. This paper proposes a method for efficiently computing the distance between a point and a curve represented as what is called a G-polynomial curve, which is a curve representation that generalizes polynomial curves to matrix Lie groups. The proposed approach exploits the structure of these curves to reduce the problem to a small number of polynomial root-finding computations. Simulation results show that the method significantly reduces computation time while maintaining accuracy compared to existing optimization-based approaches. Practical formulas are also provided for the case of the group SE(3), and the method is validated experimentally on a robotic manipulator. The methodology is implemented in a computational package, available online.

</details>


### [254] [VASO: Formally Verifiable Self-Evolving Skills for Physical AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05395)
*Yunhao Yang,Neel P. Bhatt,Kevin Wang,Samuel Tetteh,Zhangyang Wang,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了VASO框架，通过形式化验证引导大语言模型生成的机器人技能合约的自我进化，确保长期行为在未测试条件下的时间安全性，并在少于100次优化样本下实现97.2%的形式规范符合率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基础模型降低了创建可复用机器人技能的成本，但对其可靠性的验证成本仍然很高；现有方法仅提供执行层面的证据，无法保证技能在未测试条件下满足时间安全契约。

Method: VASO将每个技能表示为具有两个接口的语义契约：一个用于模型检测的形式化接口，和一个面向规划器的行为生成接口；通过模型检测器过滤逻辑不一致的技能合约，并验证技能诱导计划是否符合全局和局部时序规范；当验证失败时，将反例轨迹转化为文本梯度以更新技能合约，同时保持基础模型权重不变。

Result: 在Clearpath Jackal和PX4四旋翼无人机任务中，VASO使用不到100次优化样本即达到97.2%的形式规范符合率，优于基于执行反馈、提示优化和微调的基线方法。

Conclusion: VASO是首个将形式化验证与自我进化的LLM生成技能相结合的框架，使形式化反例成为可复用技能合约的优化反馈，而不仅仅是验证一次性计划或调整提示与模型权重。

Abstract: Reusable robot skills are becoming the basic units through which embodied agents turn open-ended instructions into long-horizon physical behavior. We argue that, while foundation models have collapsed the cost of creating these skills, the cost of trusting them has not. Existing skill-evolution loops refine skills through execution feedback, unit tests, environment reward, or LLM self-critique, but these signals provide only trace-level evidence: they show that a skill worked on sampled executions, not that skill-induced plans satisfy temporal safety contracts under untested conditions. We introduce VASO, a framework for verification-guided self-evolution of LLM-generated robot skill contracts. In VASO, each skill is represented as a semantic contract with two coupled interfaces: a formal interface that aligns robot states, observations, and control commands with logical propositions for model checking, and a planner-facing interface that guides executable behavior generation. A model checker first filters logically inconsistent skill contracts, then verifies plans induced by the skill against global and local temporal specifications. When verification fails, VASO translates the counterexample trace into a textual gradient that updates the reusable skill contract while keeping foundation-model weights frozen. On Clearpath Jackal and PX4 quadcopter tasks, VASO reaches 97.2% formal-specification compliance using fewer than 100 optimization samples, outperforming execution-feedback, prompt-optimization, and fine-tuning baselines. To our knowledge, VASO is the first framework that closes the loop between formal verification and self-evolving LLM-generated skills for physical AI agents: formal counterexamples become optimization feedback for reusable robot skill contracts, rather than merely verifying one-off plans, tuning planner prompts, or fine-tuning model weights.

</details>


### [255] [MoDex: A Diffusion Policy for Sequential Multi-Object Dexterous Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05407)
*Haofei Lu,Hongjia Liu,Yifei Dong,Florian T. Pokorny,Jens Lundell,Danica Kragic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MoDex是一种基于扩散策略的多物体连续抓取方法，通过引入对立空间条件和点云观测，在保留手部冗余自由度的同时实现高效抓取，并在仿真与真实场景中均优于现有学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统灵巧手抓取方法通常占用全部自由度于单个物体，无法为后续抓取保留冗余，限制了连续多物体抓取能力。

Method: 提出MoDex，一种扩散策略模型，直接从观测中预测下一夹持器姿态，条件化于对立空间（指定参与当前抓取的手指）和点云；采用两阶段训练：先通过模仿学习预训练，再用强化学习微调以提升性能。

Result: 在MuJoCo仿真环境及真实Franka Panda机器人搭载Allegro Hand平台上验证，MoDex在仿真和真实环境中相比基线方法成功率分别提高2.92-17.92%和6.67-17.78%。

Conclusion: MoDex能有效利用灵巧手的部分自由度完成逐次抓取，保留冗余用于后续操作，显著提升连续多物体抓取的成功率，具备良好的sim-to-real迁移能力。

Abstract: This work addresses sequentially grasping multiple objects with a single dexterous hand without releasing those already held. Most dexterous grasping methods commit all of the hand's degrees of freedom to a single object, underutilizing its dexterity and leaving no redundancy for subsequent grasps. The proposed solution, MoDex, is a diffusion policy that predicts the next gripper pose directly from observations, conditioned on an opposition space and point cloud. The opposition space condition specifies which fingers participate in the current grasp, enabling the gripper to use only a subset of its available degrees of freedom while reserving the remaining degrees of freedom for subsequent grasps. To facilitate sim-to-real transfer, MoDex is trained in two stages: first through imitation learning on expert demonstrations, and subsequently through reinforcement learning fine-tuning, which consistently improves success rates over the pre-trained policy. We evaluate MoDex in simulation on a MuJoCo-based Franka Emika Panda robot equipped with an Allegro Hand and on the corresponding real-world hardware platform. Across both simulation and real-world experiments, MoDex achieves higher success rates than the evaluated learning-based baselines, improving performance by 2.92-17.92% and 6.67-17.78%, respectively. Project page: https://modex2026.github.io/.

</details>


### [256] [Learning from Demonstrations over Riemannian Manifolds using Neural ODEs: An Extended Abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05422)
*Diana Cuervo Espinosa,Mahathi Anand,Angela P. Schoellig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于黎曼流形的演示学习方法，利用神经微分方程数值估计测地线路径，以实现机器人位置与姿态的自然运动生成，并减少传统方法的计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于机器人状态（如姿态）天然存在于非欧曲面空间中，而传统的演示学习多在欧式空间中进行，存在建模不自然的问题，因此需要在黎曼流形上进行演示学习以更好编码位置和姿态信息。

Method: 采用神经常微分方程（Neural ODEs）对黎曼流形上的测地线进行数值估计，并将生成的测地线解码回原始任务空间用于机器人执行。

Result: 仿真实验提供了该框架与其他测地线计算方法的初步比较结果，显示出在自然运动生成方面的有效性及计算效率的提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够在保持运动自然性的同时降低计算成本，具备在复杂机器人任务中应用的潜力，但仍面临流形建模与实际部署的挑战，需进一步研究。

Abstract: Learning from demonstratins (LfD) is usually performed over Euclidean spaces, while the robot state, e.g. orientation, naturally evolves over curved spaces. Therefore, to ensure natural, complex motion generation, we investigate learning from demonstrations over Riemannian manifolds that are capable of encoding both position and orientation data. Here, geodesic paths provide for natural motion between two arbitrary points within the manifold. We propose to numerically estimate geodesics via neural ordinary differential equations, mitigating large computational overhead of existing approaches. Finally, these geodesics can be decoded back into the original task space before deploying on the robot. In this extended abstract, we discuss the architecture of our framework, provide some initial insights from our simulation experiments, including comparison to other geodesic computation mechanisms, and discuss the challenges and prospects for future work.

</details>


### [257] [Uncertainty-Aware Adaptive Sensor Fusion for Autonomous Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05437)
*Simegnew Yihunie Alaba,Yuichi Motai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合深度学习与Unscented Kalman Filter的视觉惯性里程计方法，利用Vision Transformer和多尺度CNN融合IMU与视觉信息，并引入不确定性感知损失函数，在KITTI数据集上实现了高精度、高鲁棒性的姿态估计，同时保持实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升复杂环境下视觉惯性里程计（VIO）的姿态估计精度与鲁棒性，尤其是在传感器数据噪声大或不完整的情况下，需有效融合多源传感信息并建模不确定性。

Method: 采用Vision Transformer处理IMU数据以捕捉时序依赖，使用多尺度卷积神经网络（MCNN）提取基于光流的视觉运动特征，并通过自适应传感器融合模块动态加权两种模态特征；融合过程结合Unscented Kalman Filter，并设计不确定性感知损失函数，在训练中显式建模预测不确定性。

Result: 在KITTI数据集上显著优于基线方法，降低了绝对轨迹误差（ATE）和相对位姿误差（RPE），模型在NVIDIA A100 GPU上达到155 FPS，具备高效推理能力。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过深度融合学习与滤波框架，结合不确定性建模，提升了VIO系统在挑战性环境下的精度、鲁棒性与实用性，适用于资源受限的自主导航系统。

Abstract: This work introduces a hybrid deep learning approach integrated with an Unscented Kalman Filter (UKF) to enhance pose estimation accuracy in Visual-Inertial Odometry (VIO) for autonomous navigation. The proposed model employs a Vision Transformer (ViT) network to effectively capture temporal dependencies from inertial measurement unit (IMU) data and utilizes a Multiscale Convolutional Neural Network (MCNN) to learn optical flow-based motion cues from visual data. An adaptive sensor fusion module dynamically weights IMU and visual features by leveraging estimated uncertainty, thus improving robustness in diverse and challenging environmental conditions. Additionally, a novel uncertainty-aware loss function is proposed to explicitly incorporate prediction uncertainty into the learning process, enabling robust and accurate navigation under noisy, incomplete, or unreliable sensor inputs. Comprehensive evaluations of the KITTI dataset demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms baseline approaches, achieving superior performance in terms of Absolute Trajectory Error (ATE) and Relative Pose Error (RPE). The lightweight and computationally efficient model processes data at 155 FPS on an NVIDIA A100 GPU, making it highly suitable for deployment in resource-constrained autonomous systems.

</details>


### [258] [FlowPRO: Reward-Free Reinforced Fine-Tuning of Flow-Matching VLAs via Proximalized Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05468)
*Yihao Wu,He Zhang,Junbo Tan,Xueqian Wang,Zhengyou Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需奖励信号的离线强化微调框架FlowPRO，用于将视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型训练为可在真实机器人上可靠部署的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SFT、DAgger和基于奖励的强化学习方法在利用失败信号或设计真实世界奖励方面存在瓶颈，难以有效训练可靠的机器人策略。

Method: 提出了RPRO（Robotic Flow-matching Proximalized Preference Optimization），一种针对VLA模型中flow-matching动作头的偏好优化目标，并结合对比优化器与显式邻近正则化项；采用远程操作干预与回滚范式生成正负轨迹对，并通过平滑插值和批量混合转化为密集的状态级监督信号。

Result: 在四个长视野双手机器人任务上，FlowPRO取得了最高的成功率，优于四种代表性基线方法，消融实验验证了各损失组件的有效性。

Conclusion: FlowPRO通过无需奖励的设计和高效的监督生成机制，显著提升了VLA模型在真实机器人上的部署性能，解决了现有方法在奖励设计和训练稳定性方面的局限。

Abstract: Post-training Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models into policies that can be reliably deployed on real robots remains a major bottleneck. SFT and DAgger exploit failure signals only indirectly, and reward-based RL is bottlenecked by the difficulty of real-world reward design and of training reliable critics. We present FlowPRO, a reward-free offline reinforced fine-tuning framework for flow-matching VLAs. Algorithmically, we propose RPRO (Robotic Flow-matching Proximalized Preference Optimization), a preference-optimization objective tailored to the flow-matching action head of VLA models. RPRO pairs a contrastive optimizer with an explicit proximal regularizer that anchors the absolute magnitude of the implicit reward, thereby eliminating the reward-hacking failure mode of plain Flow-DPO. On the data side, a teleoperated intervention-and-rollback paradigm produces naturally paired positive and negative trajectories $(τ^w, τ^l)$ on a real robot from a single operator action; a Smooth Interpolation procedure, combined with batch mixing, then converts these sparse corrections into dense per-state supervision while preserving the base policy's capabilities. On four long-horizon bimanual tasks, FlowPRO attains the highest success rate, outperforming four representative baselines, and ablations confirm the contribution of each loss component.

</details>


### [259] [Learning Contact Representation for Leg Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05501)
*Emre Girgin,Cagri Kilic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需力传感器的自监督表示学习框架，用于腿部机器人在站立和摆动阶段的概率建模，实现了高效的接触检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖足端力传感器进行接触相位检测，但这些传感器并非普遍存在且对滑移等干扰不敏感，因此需要一种不依赖额外传感器的鲁棒方法。

Method: 利用关节编码器的标准传感器集，构建自监督表示学习框架，通过学习到的表示对站立相和摆动相进行概率建模。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法优于需要传感器增强和标注的监督方法以及基线概率方法，在接触检测中表现出更高的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的自监督接触检测框架在不依赖力传感器的情况下仍能实现高精度的相位识别，具有良好的通用性和鲁棒性，并已公开代码。

Abstract: The estimation of odometry in legged robots depends on the assumption that the velocity of the foot with respect to the world remains zero during the stance phase. Feedback for the main body velocity is derived from the kinematic serial chain of the feet making accurate leg phase detection is a critical subproblem. A considerable number of studies employ ground reaction force sensors mounted at the tip of the foot to classify, yet these sensors may not be universally available for all legged robots. Additionally, these sensors are often unresponsive to unaccounted disturbances, such as slippage, while the foot remains in contact with the ground. In this study, we propose a self-supervised representation learning framework for contact detection that utilizes the standard sensor set of joint encoders without reliance on force sensor augmentations. We employ learned representations to model the stance and swing phases probabilistically. The experimental results obtained confirm the efficacy of the proposed self-supervised contact detector. Our framework exhibited superior performance in comparison to supervised methods which necessitate sensor set augmentation and labeling, as well as baseline probabilistic approaches. Additionally, we make our code available to the public.

</details>


### [260] [Auditing Demonstration Curation Metrics: Action-Only Scorers Fail on the Structural Defects That Degrade Imitation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05588)
*Aarav Bedi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个受控测试平台，系统评估了七种演示数据筛选指标在两种缺陷类型下的表现，发现仅基于动作的指标无法检测结构性错误，甚至可能产生反向效果，而结合状态轨迹的指标虽能检测但恢复效果有限，高检测准确率并不保证下游性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同验证方式下的演示数据筛选指标难以直接比较，且不清楚哪些指标能有效识别损害策略质量的低质演示，因此需要一个统一的受控环境来系统评估这些指标的真实有效性。

Method: 构建一个可注入已知类型缺陷（细微扰动和结构性错误）的受控测试平台，评估七种筛选指标在区分缺陷与正常演示方面的能力，并检验使用这些指标筛选后的数据训练行为克隆策略对任务成功率的影响。

Result: 细微扰动可通过多变量离群值检测识别并有效剔除，从而完全恢复性能差距；而结构性错误对所有仅基于动作的指标不可见，其中两个指标反而给缺陷演示更高评分，导致策略性能不升反降；只有考察状态轨迹的指标能检测结构性错误，但最佳情况也仅恢复三分之一的性能差距。
